# AI prompt log, 2026-11

Format and rules: see `.claude/CLAUDE.md`. From 2026-11-01, code is worked, reviewed and deployed on a local
`dev` branch, and GitHub main gets at most two squashed commits a day. Each entry cites the dev commits and the
squash commit that carries them. The full dev-to-squash map is `research-log/dev-squash-map.txt`.

> worker killed during the wait leaves a record that reconcile can book; (h) pass the caller's price into the stop
> place/restore calls so tick rounding can keep a SELL stop below the market.

> replay_spill.

  double booking after one failure); these were fixed. Re-reviews of each follow-up came back SAFE: the
  quantity-aware repair, fill-wait SELLs counted as pending, the guarded late mark, and the one-day bound.
- Five reviews in all. On dev, not deployed yet; the deploy is planned for the 11-03 evening stop.

## 2026-11-03 10:39 EST (first commit): Stop restores and coverage fixes over a slow or refused cancel (M230 and follow-ups)

**Luke (original, verbatim):**
> if you finish all actionable items start to think and research about how we could make this bot better, and then act on it without me involved.

(Professor pre-deploy review item M230, plus follow-ups from fresh agent reviews and the professor's add-on review; written by trading-bot-ee, Claude.)

**Request drafted by:** AI, under the standing instruction quoted above (the item named in the refined block). Luke's words are the instruction, not this specific request. Relabelled 2026-11-06 (professor M317).

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: a restore over a stop whose cancel is slow, or that reverts, must not leave a double stop or a bare position. _fix_overstop must never count a cancelling stop as cover. A cross-client stop that refuses a cancel must not cause a cancel/replace loop. Alerts must not hide a safety 🚨. A cancel must never hit another ticker's order with the same orderId.
> Scope / files: execution.py, tasks.py, risk_manager.py, ibkr.py, main.py, tests.
> Constraints: under-cover is worse than over-cover; no threshold or risk-limit change; old Celery messages stay compatible.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the policy for a refused stop larger than the position (packet H22, Luke).
> Acceptance check: suite passes; each commit reviewed SAFE by a fresh agent; professor pre-deploy review SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: H22 (options only).

**Done:**
- The restore re-reads after 5 s when a stop is cancelling, and sizes to min(cache, fresh).
- A new tasks.recheck_stop_coverage runs under the ticker lock: up to 6 tries, with a fresh-vs-cache position cross-check.
- _fix_overstop waits for both cancelling statuses and sizes from working stops only.
- Stops that refuse a cancel are remembered by permId (stop_cancel_refused_<T>), so they aren't cancelled again.
- Refused stops larger than the position get an unthrottled 🚨 and one cancel retry.
- The alert throttles are split.
- cancel_order checks symbol and permId.
- The PT-hold wording is fixed.
- An order-dependent test is fixed.
- One version (8966f40) was blocked in review for a cancel/replace loop and fixed before merge.

- execution.py (AI-edited: `_restore_cover`, `_restore_sized_stop`, `_restore_stop_after_failed_sell`; `_queue_coverage_recheck` AI-generated).

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Stop coverage re-check, flat and sign-flip reads

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; findings from the professor's 11:40 add-on review (m327, m329-m331), the 12:12 review (m339) and run 42 at 12:47 (m345, m354)):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: no SELL stop is ever placed on a short. A fresh read that leaves out a ticker or disagrees on sign must not size a stop from the cache, and the alert must say what happened.
> Scope / files: execution.py, tasks.py, risk_manager.py, tests.
> Constraints: under-cover is worse than over-cover; no stop-level or risk-limit change.
> Out of scope (do not touch): M237, the policy for a refused stop larger than the position (Luke, packet H22).
> Acceptance check: tests for flat, missing-ticker and sign-flip reads; professor SAFE.

**Done:**
- The coverage re-check does nothing on a fresh read that leaves out the ticker and queues another look within the cap. Reads that disagree on sign size to 0.
- _restore_sized_stop places nothing and returns "sign_flip" when a fresh read shows a short. That sends the throttled sign-flip alert and queues the restore. The queued retry and reconcile's auto-stop check the sign before any SELL stop. The give-up alert says to check long or short in TWS.
- Reviewed SAFE (12:12 and run 42).

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Vector store resume, dead-letter and canary (continues the 11-03 09:47 01n entry)

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; findings from the professor's 11:40 review (M238, M239), the 12:12 review (M240, m335-m338, m340, m341) and run 42 (m344, m347)):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: the 3-failure bound on rebuild resumes holds even when a process is killed mid-upsert. One bad record can no longer pause prune forever, and a store that takes no writes never sets records aside.
> Scope / files: vector_store.py, tasks.py, tests.
> Constraints: no document is dropped silently (set-asides go to a dead-letter file and alert); retrieval for the analyst unchanged.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the resume age filter. It was built, then removed, because it changes analyst input (Luke's).
> Acceptance check: tests for kills, progress, isolated failures, canary, cap; professor SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:**
- Every resume is counted before it starts and reset on progress (records landed).
- A record failing three resumes in a row is dead-lettered, but only when the store is shown to take writes: another upsert landed, or a scratch canary record (written, read back, deleted) passes. At most min(10, max(1, n//100)) are set aside per run. A final set-aside check runs once when resumes stop.
- The canary is never searched, rebuilt or kept. Set-asides alert once a day per collection, and the held counts are flushed.

**Files changed:** `vector_store.py` (AI-edited: `_resume_attempt`, `rebuild_collection`, `prune_old_documents`, `pending_rebuilds`, `search`, `_read_collection_docs`, `_count_collection_rows`; AI-generated: `_past_max_age`, `_age_cutoff`, `_deadletter_path`, `_dead_letter`, `_canary_upsert`, `_isolated_cap`, `_fsync_dir`, `_set_aside_after_giving_up`, `PRUNE_MAX_AGE_DAYS`), `tasks.py` (AI-edited: `_log_vector_store_rebuild`, `_rebuild_and_log`, `prune_vector_store`, `_alert_unfinished_rebuilds`; AI-generated: `_alert_set_aside`), tests (AI-edited): `tests/test_vector_store_rebuild_recovery.py`, `tests/test_vector_store_single_view.py`

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Buy rules, a 30% micro-tier per-name cap and a screener new_fact buy gate

**Request (drafted by Jarvis, claude-jarvis@pc, bus #186 and #189, relaying Luke's decisions; not Luke's own words. Summary, not the bus text):**
> #186: on the micro tier, cap any one name at 30% of equity for new buys (never sell to get under it). Add a new_fact field to the 7B screener output (new factual information vs opinion or recap), and let an OPEN/ADD route only when a screener new fact is behind it.
> #189: gate scope news_only; window 24h.

(This follows audit 2026-11-03 15:23 findings 2 and 3: the 11-03 buy came from an opinion piece, and the single-name cap was unenforced.)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: build both buy rules as Luke set them, applied wherever a buy is sized or re-sized, with a route row for every block.
> Scope / files: risk_manager.py, agents/crew.py, agents/screener.py (prompt and parser), agents/c6_shadow.py, config.py, db.py, tasks.py, helios3d/replay/harness.py, tests.

> Out of scope (do not touch): other tiers; trims of existing holdings.

**Done:**
- micro_name_cap cuts or blocks a micro-tier OPEN/ADD (ROUTE_MICRO_NAME_CAP). The screener records new_fact. new_fact_gate blocks a buy without one (ROUTE_NO_NEW_FACT), with defaults news_only and 24h per #189.
- Review fixes: M256 (the held read fails closed, including a stop record with no broker position, which alerts once a day), M257 (SWAP INCOMPLETE alert after a post-sale block, 0.5 pp swap slack), M259 (a typo keeps the gate on and alerts once), plus m421, m422 and m425.

## 2026-11-03 21:53 EST (commit): Outcome close row locking; orphan sweep skips in-flight sells

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; follow-up (b) in roadmap 01zg (the close_trade_outcome open-rows SELECT has no FOR UPDATE), then m462 and m463 from the professor's reviews of next-2026-11-07):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: two concurrent closes of the same ticker can't overwrite each other. A close that loses a lock race is retried. The orphan sweep doesn't flag a row whose real close is in flight.
> Scope / files: db.py `close_trade_outcome`, `flag_orphan_outcomes`, tests.
> Constraints: no row lock or pooled connection held during the SPY price fetch; failures return False, never raise.
> Out of scope (do not touch): m465 (a queued flat exit waiting over 30 minutes; an orders-row write failure), left open.
> Acceptance check: tests; professor SAFE (ledger 44e-44h).
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:**
- close_trade_outcome looks at the open rows unlocked, fetches the SPY exit price, then locks them (FOR UPDATE) and updates only rows still open. It retries once after a deadlock or serialization failure and rolls back quietly.
- The orphan sweep skips tickers with a SELL filled or submitted in the last 30 minutes, or still trade_pending.

**Files changed:** `db.py` (AI-edited: `close_trade_outcome`, `flag_orphan_outcomes`), `tests/test_close_outcome_locking.py` (AI-generated), `tests/test_tasks_dedup.py` (AI-edited)

**Commit:** `08cc7b1`, `db66097`, `b587bd9`, `27e7e5c`, `da0ece0`

**Deploy:** next-2026-11-07 `da0ece0`, planned live from 11-09 05:00 (pending a decision on the weekend loads)

## 2026-11-04 17:04 EST (commit): A/B resume check, stale-quote None fix, concentration record

**Request (professor review run 49 (trading-bot-aa, an AI reviewer agent), relayed on the bus at 16:47; worked under the improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke):**
> M290: the runner never compares a resume's config (model_digest, ollama_version, options, tool_sha) with the first run line ... M288: execution.py:1512-1517. If the first quote is stale and the last of the 3 retries returns None, pq becomes None. The stale-BUY defer/abort and the 8% drift check are then both skipped ... M289: the persisted concentration flag still writes "position X% exceeds cap" using max(target, order) ... crew.py doesn't rescale sizing["order_pct"] after the cap ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: (1) a check that refuses a mismatched A/B resume without anyone reading arm data; (2) the stale-BUY guard and drift check survive a None quote; (3) the stored sizing says which weight the concentration check used and what the cap left.
> Scope / files: scripts/g33_resume_check.py (new, dev), execution.py and agents/crew.py (run47-plans), tests.
> Constraints: (1) must not change the runner (tool_sha hashes it, so an edit would split the resume) and reads only kind "run" lines; (2) no new block condition: with no priced quote at all, use the latest price as the existing exception path does; (3) flag text unchanged (downstream readers), record-only keys plus the order_pct rescale; nothing reaches the brief.
> Out of scope (do not touch): G33 design, sample or decision rules; stale-price retry policy (G49); cap values (#183/G36).
> Acceptance check: new tests fail on the old code and pass on the new; full suite passes; professor review.
> Open questions for Luke: none (whether a missing quote should defer like a stale one is a follow-up once logged).

**Done:**
- g33_resume_check.py compares run lines with the first and, with --preflight, the config a run started now would record. It exits 2 on a mismatch.
- A None on a stale re-check now keeps the last quote. With no priced quote at all, the drift check falls back to ibkr.get_latest_price and logs a warning.
- risk_json.concentration_check records checked_pct, target_pct, order_pct, cap_pct, shares_before, shares_after and order_pct_after. order_pct is now rescaled with the capped shares.

**Files changed:**
- `scripts/g33_resume_check.py` (AI-generated)
- `tests/test_g33_resume_check.py` (AI-generated)
- `execution.py` (AI-edited: priced-quote re-check loop in `execute_approved`)
- `tests/test_execution.py` (AI-edited: two tests in `TestStalePriceBuyGuard`)
- `agents/crew.py` (AI-edited: concentration block of `_route_to_approval`)
- `tests/test_concentration_record.py` (AI-generated)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: after a TRIM or manual SELL, the broker stop has already been cancelled. If the sell part-fills, the code re-placed a stop for owned - filled while the rest of the sell was still working (over-stop once it fills); if the stop record read returned None (missing, or a DB error read non-strict), no branch ran and the remaining shares had no stop, no alert and no retry. Route both cases to the existing reconcile re-protect (which subtracts a working sell) and alert.
> Scope / files: execution.py (execute_approved TRIM branch, execute_manual SELL branch), tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py (run47-plans).
> Constraints: EXIT's part-fill handling unchanged; a full fill with a readable record still re-places at once; strict record read.

> Acceptance check: part-filled TRIM and manual sell queue RESTORE and don't place; missing and unreadable records queue RESTORE and alert; a full fill with a record still places for the remainder; full suite passes.
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:** both paths read the record strictly; a part fill or a missing/unreadable record queues tasks._queue_reprotect(RESTORE, order id) and sends one alert; execute_manual's local_order_id is initialised before its try. Five tests. Suite 4225 passed.

**Files changed:** `execution.py` (AI-edited: execute_approved TRIM stop block, execute_manual SELL stop block), `tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py` (AI-generated)

Follow-up `4428661` (run47-plans, professor 62j and an SEF question): the queued restore reads a fresh price for the floor check and passes it to the stop placement, skipping the floor with no price; the floor applies after a profit target's stop and survives a merge into a queued target (`tasks._reprotect_after_late_sell`, `tasks._queue_reprotect`, AI-edited); tests added in `tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py` (AI-generated). ## 2026-11-08 17:05 EST: Telegram-first shutdown, stop budget, bounded IB qualify, stop-lock socket timeouts

**Trigger:** limitations audit 2026-11-08 15:08, items #5 and #8. A review subagent then flagged that the first version's 15 s join was shorter than an approval, and that joining the long-polling poller wasted the wait.

**Request drafted by:** audit findings #5 and #8 (AI). Luke's standing permission covers changes like this.

**Open for Luke:** none. No root change was needed: the LLM grace dropped from 420 to 390 s instead of raising TimeoutStopSec.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: no approval starts during a stop, a running one gets to finish before exit, and the whole stop fits TimeoutStopSec=480. IB contract qualification and stop-lock Redis calls can't hang forever.
> Scope / files: main.py, process_manager.py, ibkr.py, stop_lock.py, tests.
> Constraints: never raise in shutdown; keep stop-lock fail-open; keep orders-last (M57).
> Out of scope: unit-file edits (need root), order logic.
> Acceptance check: new tests including the budget check against the unit file, full suite green, fresh subagent review.
> Open questions for Luke: none

**Done:** `_shutdown` now calls `_stop_telegram_polling()` first, then `stop_all`, then `_join_telegram_worker` against a deadline 60 s from the start of the stop. Only the worker thread is joined, not the poller. The LLM grace is 390 s, so the worst case is 5 + 390 + 60 = 455 s. Pool children drain the commission writer at exit (`ibkr.drain_commission_queue`). `_stock` qualifies through `_bounded_req`. The stop-lock Redis client gets 5 s socket and connect timeouts, and a timeout takes the existing fail-open path.

**Files changed:** `main.py` (AI-edited: _TELEGRAM_JOIN_S, _stop_telegram_polling, _join_telegram_worker, _shutdown), `process_manager.py` (AI-edited: _CELERY_GRACE_S and comment), `ibkr.py` (AI-edited: _commission_writer task_done, drain_commission_queue, _stock), `tasks.py` (AI-edited: _drain_on_child_exit commission drain), `stop_lock.py` (AI-edited: _REDIS_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_S, _redis), `tests/test_shutdown_order.py`, `tests/test_qualify_bounded.py`, `tests/test_stop_lock_socket_timeout.py` (AI-generated), `tests/test_process_manager_stop.py`, `tests/test_child_exit_drain.py` (AI-edited)

**Done:** `stop_all(before_orders=)` hook. Shutdown waits there for the telegram-worker thread, until 180 s after the stop began. ## 2026-11-10 15:07 EST: an ADD read as a fresh entry when the position cache lagged the fill

**Trigger:** the 15-minute monitor at 15:07. Approval #171, an NVDA ADD, filled and logged "BUY filled with a stale stop record".

**Request drafted by:** monitor finding (AI).

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: an ADD gets one stop for the whole position even when the portfolio cache hasn't caught up with the fill.
> Scope / files: ibkr.py (new get_fresh_stock_position), execution.py (_whole_position_stop, execute_approved call, _alert_prior_stop_unread), tests.
> Constraints: order/stop path, so a fresh review is required before commit; never cancel a stop on an uncertain read; deploy only while stopped.
> Out of scope: the live NVDA stops placed at 15:07 (both shares covered; left as they are); whether the ADD should have happened (Luke's: 01zb, the new-fact tone rule).
> Acceptance check: tests for the lagging cache, an empty cache, a short read, an OPEN, a failed read and a missing cost; full suite green; review SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none for this build

**Done:**
- _whole_position_stop read ib.portfolio(), which still showed the pre-fill quantity. It treated the ADD as a fresh entry: a separate 1-share stop @ $146.24, the old stop @ $163.68 left working, and the record rewritten to $146.24.
- For an ADD whose cache shows no earlier shares, a fresh reqPositions() read now decides. A long quantity above the fill gets one whole-position stop at max(new, old). A failed read, or no average cost, keeps the old stop and record, stops only the new shares, and sends the check-stops alert with the cause.
- Review: SAFE after fixes. OPENs never take the fresh read (partial fills), short reads are not adds, an empty cache falls through, and a failed read keeps the record. Suite: 4608 passed on dev and on run47-plans (with yfinance 1.8.0).

**Files changed:** `ibkr.py` (AI-edited: get_fresh_stock_position AI-generated), `execution.py` (AI-edited: _whole_position_stop, execute_approved call, _alert_prior_stop_unread), `tests/test_execution.py` (AI-edited), `tests/test_stop_restore_sizing.py` (AI-edited)

**Commit:** `b35cd8a` (dev); the same change is `d4ab736` on run47-plans.

## 2026-11-12 11:23 EST: book untracked partial exits (roadmap 04z)

**Trigger:** limitations audit 2026-11-12 11:23. At the 09:30 open the first of NVDA's two stop lots filled (the broker position went from 2 to 1). Helios wrote no trade row and sent no alert: the stale-stop path books an exit only once the whole position is gone, and the 17:45 ledger reconcile is log-only.

**Request drafted by:** audit finding (AI). It records an execution that already happened; nothing is sent to the broker.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: when the broker still holds a long but the trade ledger's net is above it, book each SELL execution the bot did not place (a resting stop lot or a manual sale) once, with a trade row and a Telegram alert; the later full-exit path must not book those shares again.
> Scope / files: tasks.py (reconcile body, stale-stop path), db.py (_NOT_EXECUTION_TRADE_REASONS), tests/test_partial_stop_fills.py.
> Constraints: record-only; never raises; no booking while a bot SELL is pending, while position reads disagree, before the ticker's latest ledger BUY, or within 5 minutes of the execution; the state write is verified before the trade row; fresh review of the stop-path code before deploy; deploy only after 16:00 with a drained restart (IBKR returns same-day executions only, so the 11-12 fill must be booked today).
> Out of scope: any order change; changing what a stop_slippage row means (Luke's); replay_spill --apply (Luke's).
> Acceptance check: new tests including the NVDA 11-12 case; full suite green; after deploy, the next reconcile books the NVDA 09:30 SELL.
> Open questions for Luke: none for this build. Whether partial exits should also get their own stop_slippage rows is listed as an option on roadmap 04z.

**Done:**
- Reconcile books each unrecorded SELL execution per permId (today-scoped state), with reason "Partial exit: …" and a "PARTIAL EXIT" Telegram alert. The stale-stop exit nets those shares out of its trade row; its trade outcome and stop_slippage rows still cover the whole exit.
- "Partial exit%" rows are excluded from trade_logged_near, like the other non-execution rows.
- Three rounds of fresh review (round 1 blocked; rounds 2 and 3 fixes applied). One documented gap: a spilled booking replayed by hand could double-book a bot trim (roadmap 04z).
- Suite: 4641 passed on dev.

**Files changed:** `tasks.py` (AI-edited: _et_today, _partial_booked_read, _net_of_partial_bookings and _book_partial_stop_fills AI-generated; reconcile call site and stale-stop netting AI-edited), `db.py` (AI-edited: _NOT_EXECUTION_TRADE_REASONS), `tests/test_partial_stop_fills.py` (AI-generated), `tests/test_order_row_before_fill_wait.py` (AI-edited: pinned exclusion list)

**Commit:** `87eb539` (dev); the same change is `92ea06f` on run47-plans.

**Deploy note (2026-11-12 16:11 EST):** the live clone was fast-forwarded from `a7f301c` to run47-plans `92ea06f` (tag `deploy-2026-11-12`) after the close, with workers idle, followed by a planned restart of helios-local. The 16:15 reconcile, the first on the new code, booked the 09:30 NVDA stop-lot execution as trade row 133 (SELL 1 @ 151.17, price_source fill) and sent the PARTIAL EXIT Telegram alert. The row's timestamp is the booking time, because log_trade takes no timestamp; the execution time is 14:30:11 UTC. The other reconciled exits have the same limit.

## 2026-11-13 14:41 EST: count each broker position once in fresh position reads (roadmap 04ae)

**Trigger:** Telegram + error monitor, 14:41. At 14:28 an NVDA ADD (approval #173) filled 1 share, so the broker held 2. The post-fill fresh read returned 4. The resting 1-share stop was cancelled, and the whole-position stop for 4 was refused ("broker shows 2"). NVDA had no stop from 14:28:51 until reconcile placed one for 2 shares at 14:30:54.

**Request drafted by:** monitor finding (AI). Stop-path fix; it changes no trading decision.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: a fresh reqPositions() read counts each position once, so a whole-position stop after an ADD matches the shares held.
> Scope / files: ibkr.py (get_fresh_stock_positions, get_fresh_stock_position, _refuse_sell_on_short's confirmation read), tests/test_fresh_position_dedupe.py.
> Constraints: same results when there are no duplicate rows; never count more than the broker holds; fresh review before deploy; deploy only after the close with a drained restart.
> Out of scope: the add-to-a-loser rule and blocking adds before earnings (Luke's); the separate ADD stop-path gaps found by the review (roadmap 04af).
> Acceptance check: new tests reproduce the 2-read-as-4 case and the sell-guard case; full suite green; review verdict SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none for this fix

**Done:**
- Cause: ib_async appends every position message received during reqPositions() to the result. TWS sent NVDA twice (old and new average cost), and the code summed both rows.
- New ibkr._latest_positions keeps the last row per (account, conId). It is used in both fresh-read helpers and in the sell guard's confirmation read. In the sell guard, an over-count could have allowed an oversized SELL.
- Fresh review: SAFE. Suite: 4646 passed on dev.

**Files changed:** `ibkr.py` (AI-edited: _latest_positions AI-generated; get_fresh_stock_positions, get_fresh_stock_position, _refuse_sell_on_short read sites), `tests/test_fresh_position_dedupe.py` (AI-generated)

**Commit:** `47515b4` (dev); the same change is `7ba0857` on run47-plans.

**Deploy note (2026-11-13 16:11 EST):** the live clone was fast-forwarded from `92ea06f` to run47-plans `7ba0857` (tag `deploy-2026-11-13`) after the close, with the workers idle, followed by a planned restart of helios-local.

## 2026-11-13 14:45 EST: close the ADD whole-position stop's unstopped windows (roadmap 04af)

**Trigger:** the fresh review of 04ae (14:4x) found more paths where an ADD cancels the old stop and then places one that leaves shares bare.

**Request drafted by:** review finding (AI). Stop-path fix; no trading decision changes.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: an ADD replaces the old stop with one whole-position stop only when the whole position is known; otherwise keep the old stop and record and stop only the filled shares, with an alert.
> Scope / files: execution.py (_whole_position_stop, _held_before_order, execute_approved stop block, _alert_prior_stop_unread), tests/test_execution.py, tests/test_stop_restore_sizing.py.
> Constraints: never cancel and then under- or over-size; the common case (reads agree) still gives one whole-position stop; fresh review per revision; deploy only after the close.
> Out of scope: buy-gate changes (the add-to-loser rule, the earnings window, the sector regime): Luke's.
> Acceptance check: tests for each disagreement case and a caller-level test; full suite green; final review SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none for this fix

**Done:**
- Every ADD runs a fresh reqPositions read. The old stop is cancelled only when the reads include the new shares on top of the count held just before the order (`_held_before_order`, read from the cache before the order goes out).
- A failed read, reads that disagree, or reads short of holding+fill keep the old stop and record, stop only the filled shares, leave the kept record's order id alone, and send the reworded check-stops alert.
- An early-reconcile kick was tried and reverted after review: it started the wrong task, and kicking the right one would shorten the retry grace periods.
- Four fresh reviews (the last SAFE). Suite: 4664 passed on dev and on run47-plans.

**Files changed:** `execution.py` (AI-edited: _held_before_order AI-generated; _whole_position_stop ADD branch, execute_approved stop block and held_before_order, _alert_prior_stop_unread wording), `tests/test_execution.py` (AI-edited: new TestAddStopsWholePosition cases), `tests/test_stop_restore_sizing.py` (AI-edited: held_before pass-through and caller test)

**Commit:** `fc86893` (dev, squash of branch add-stop-gaps); the same change is `b0ddb02` on run47-plans.

**Deploy note (2026-11-13 17:28 EST):** the live clone was fast-forwarded from `7ba0857` to `b0ddb02` (tag `deploy-2026-11-13b`) after the close with the workers idle, followed by a planned restart of helios-local.

## 2026-11-14 04:48 EST: stop an OPEN on an already-held ticker as an ADD (roadmap 04af follow-up)

**Trigger:** the open item left on roadmap 04af: an OPEN that fills on a ticker the broker already holds had its stop decided from the lagging portfolio cache alone.

**Request drafted by:** roadmap 04af open item (AI), worked by the improve loop. Stop-path fix; no trading decision changes.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: an OPEN whose ticker was already held before the order gets the same stop handling as an ADD (fresh read; keep the old stop when the reads are ambiguous). Fresh OPENs behave as before.
> Scope / files: execution.py (_held_before_order, _whole_position_stop, the held_before_order read in execute_approved), tests/test_execution.py, tests/test_stop_restore_sizing.py, tests/test_stop_record_write_failure.py.
> Constraints: only the count read before the order may reclassify an OPEN (a still-filling OPEN can show more than its fill in a post-fill read); fresh review; deploy only while stopped.
> Out of scope: the late-fill path in tasks.py (separate follow-up); buy-gate changes (Luke's).
> Acceptance check: tests for each held/fill/read case; full suite green; fresh review SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none for this fix

**Done:**
- `_held_before_order` is read for OPENs too and returns 0.0 when the cache shows no position. An OPEN with shares held before the order takes the ADD stop path.
- A reclassified OPEN whose reads show exactly the fill, with a different pre-order count, is booked as a fresh entry over a leftover record (the old shares were sold by a stop fill the cache hadn't dropped). Reads below the fill, or a pre-order count equal to the fill, stay unknown.
- Two fresh reviews (the first found the leftover-record regression, the second SAFE with one tightening, applied). Suite: 4674 passed on dev and on run47-plans. Follow-up noted: the late-fill path still treats such an OPEN's late shares as a fresh entry (pre-existing).

**Files changed:** `execution.py` (AI-edited: _held_before_order flat case and docstring; _whole_position_stop OPEN reclassification and open_over_leftover; execute_approved held_before_order read for OPEN), `tests/test_execution.py` (AI-edited: new TestAddStopsWholePosition OPEN cases, _held_before_order test), `tests/test_stop_restore_sizing.py` (AI-edited: caller test parametrized over ADD and OPEN), `tests/test_stop_record_write_failure.py` (AI-edited: _entry pins held_before to None)

**Commit:** `8d31944` (dev); the same change is `2b0d52c` on run47-plans.

**Deploy note (2026-11-14 05:00 EST):** the live clone was fast-forwarded from `b0ddb02` to `2b0d52c` (tag `deploy-2026-11-14`) while helios-local was stopped for the weekend; no restart. helios-standby doesn't import execution.py. The bot loads the change at its next start, Monday 2026-11-16 ~05:00.

## 2026-11-15 08:11 EST: fix the red GitHub CI (dependency install)

**Trigger:** agent-bus #390 from claude@pc (a peer agent; Luke didn't type it): every Helios commit shows a failed "Tests" run on GitHub; the job fails at "Install dependencies"; asked for the cause and a CI-only fix.

**Request drafted by:** claude@pc (AI, peer agent on the bus), relaying that Luke noticed the red X. Not Luke's own words.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: make the GitHub "Tests" workflow install dependencies and run pytest again.
> Scope / files: .github/workflows/tests.yml only.
> Constraints: CI only; don't change requirements.txt or the box's venv; don't touch the live deploy.
> Out of scope: upgrading or replacing polygon-api-client; the box's own dependency set.
> Acceptance check: a dry-run resolve for Python 3.12 succeeds; the next push to main shows the workflow green.
> Open questions for Luke: none

**Done:**
- Cause (from `gh run view 43620380412 --log-failed`): pip's resolver can't satisfy requirements.txt. polygon-api-client caps certifi<2026 (and 1.13.0, where it backtracks to, caps websockets<12), while yfinance>=1.8.0 needs curl_cffi>=0.17, which needs certifi>=2026.10.19. The box's venv has polygon-api-client 1.16.3 beside certifi 2026.10.23 (installed in that order), so the bot runs; a fresh resolve can't.
- The workflow installs requirements.txt without the polygon line, then `polygon-api-client==1.16.3` with `--no-deps`, matching the box. Dry-run resolve for Python 3.12 succeeds.
- Not changed: requirements.txt (a fresh install of it on a new machine hits the same conflict; noted for later).

**Files changed:** `.github/workflows/tests.yml` (AI-edited: the "Install dependencies" step)

**Commit:** `7e4ddd6` (dev); goes to GitHub main with the next squash push.

**Done:** added `_entry_gate`, called after the two shadows in `_route_to_approval`, which now return what they read. It blocks an OPEN/ADD in two cases:
- a broker exit under `STOPOUT_REENTRY_GATE_DAYS` (10) days old;
- a last completed close below both SMA50 and SMA200, on bars at most 5 days old.

Failed reads and stale bars do not block (fail open), and conviction does not override. I chose the parameters under Luke's delegation:
- both averages rather than either, so only an established downtrend is blocked;
- 10 days, the widest stop-out shadow window, because both live stop-out re-entries lost.

## 2026-11-21 16:28 EST: trend gate applies only in a market downtrend

**Trigger:** the team gap hunt by peer session trading-bot-4e, assigned under Luke's 11-21 delegation ("You have three new opus 5.5 medium peers... build and implement/restart without me, you have ongoing permission"; earlier the same day: "well its up to you").
**Request drafted by:** trading-bot-ee (AI) from the gap hunt (helios-research "08 Helios Engineering/team-gap-hunt-2026-11-21.md", finding 1). In June-October 2026, buys made below both the 50- and 200-day averages did better over 20 sessions than buys above both (+7.0% vs -1.5%, n=85/184; pilot diagnostics). The 11-23 trend gate would have blocked that bucket.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: keep the trend gate's protection in a market downtrend without blocking pullback buys in an up-market.
> Scope / files: agents/crew.py (_entry_gate), config.py, tests/test_entry_gate.py, ARCHITECTURE.md, LIVE.md.
> Constraints: fail open on a failed, stale or short market read; the stop-out gate is unchanged; review before load; load while stopped before the 11-23 05:00 start.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the stop-out cooldown, sells, sizing, the analyst prompt.
> Acceptance check: a stock below both averages is blocked only while SPY closes below its own 200-day; the market read is recorded on the row; suite green; review clean.
> Open questions for Luke: none (delegated).

**Done:** _entry_gate reads SPY's cached trend (one read, made only when the stock itself would be blocked) and blocks only when SPY's last completed close is below its 200-day SMA on fresh bars. decision.entry_gate.trend.market records the read. The design was chosen after seeing the data: it is the standard market-level 200-day filter, and it is consistent with both the June-October history (market up) and the November live losses (SPY below its 200-day since 11-20; the history has no buys in that state). On 11-21 SPY was 5.5% below its 200-day, so Monday's behaviour is unchanged.

A fresh Opus 5.5 reviewer found it SAFE-WITH-FIXES. Fix applied: the market read sits in its own try, so a failure there cannot also drop a stop-out block. Missing tests were added. Suite 5015 passed. Merged into run47-plans (ee766bd) and pulled into the live clone at 16:35 while stopped. The tag deploy-2026-11-23 was moved before its first load.

**Files changed:** `agents/crew.py` (AI-edited: the market condition in `_entry_gate`), `config.py` (AI-edited: `ENTRY_TREND_GATE_MARKET_TICKER`), `tests/test_entry_gate.py` (AI-edited: `TestMarketCondition`, fixture default), `ARCHITECTURE.md` (AI-edited: entry-gate paragraph), `LIVE.md` (AI-edited: deploy rows)

> You have three new opus 5.5 medium peers, these are your "employees" so to speak. You can delegate work to them as you wish. Try not to slow down as much as you have. inform jarvis of this change so he is aware.

**Request drafted by:** trading-bot-13 (AI), from the SNDK root-cause read during the split-guard work. The nightly refresh rewrites only one split-adjusted year, so rows older than that keep the pre-split scale. Blast radius: SNDK 8:1, MNST and APH 2:1.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: (A) the nightly refresh detects a split-basis change between fetched and stored closes and, when switched on, rescales the stored rows the fetch does not overwrite, in the same transaction as the upsert. (B) A repair script finds and fixes steps already stored.
> Scope / files: ingestion/price_history.py, db.py, config.py, new scripts/rescale_stale_head.py, tests, ARCHITECTURE.md.
> Constraints:
> - A ships record-only (PRICE_CACHE_RESCALE_ENABLED = False: a price_cache_rescale row with applied false and the plain upsert).
> - A rescale needs a median ratio within 2% of a split factor, with at least 90% of shared dates within 2% of it.
> - It runs under an advisory lock and re-checks a stored close before writing.
> - A failed or raising write skips that night's upsert.
> - B is a dry run by default and refuses a step that no longer matches.
> - Ratios and counts only.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the split guard, the trend gate, any other table.
> Acceptance check: split factors are detected, dividend-sized gaps ignored, partial adjustments reported as unexplained, one transaction or none; B's dry run is read-only; suite green; review clean.
> Open questions for Luke: none (a data-integrity fix). The data change itself is logged in 01 Data/CHANGELOG.md.

**Done:** The fresh review found no blockers in A (S1 and S2 fixed in af1540d, along with all the nits) and found B safe. The lead ran B on 11-21 with the bot stopped: SNDK 91 rows × 1/8, and MNST 29 and APH 34 rows × 1/2. The row counts matched the dry run. Afterwards, SNDK's Trend Template read shows no step, and its RS proxy uses the true trailing-year return.

**Request drafted by:** trading-bot-13 (AI), from trading-bot-26's read-only table of every place that sizes a SELL STP's quantity, made during add-stop-qty. Row 14, the profit-target remainder, sizes from the cache seconds after a sale and is the most refusal-prone. Row 5, the reconcile late fill, has no expect_at_least, unlike row 4.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: (row 14) size the profit-target remainder stop from this process's broker count, capped at held minus the sale; (row 5) record whether the late-BUY re-protect would wait for the cache to reach the broker count.
> Scope / files: risk_manager.py (`_pt_stop_qty`, `RiskManager._partial_sell_and_resize_stop`), tasks.py (the late-fill expectation helpers, `_book_late_fill`, `_reprotect_after_late_sell`, `_queue_reprotect`, `_retry_pending_reprotects`, `reconcile_unconfirmed_orders`), ibkr.py (`get_broker_stock_qty` settle), config.py, tests.
> Constraints:

> - Every row-14 count is capped at held − fill. The cap is silent when the count is stale by exactly the sale; otherwise it sends an info alert and sets capped_ambiguous.
> - Every PT alert is sent after `manage_stop_place`, not inside the cancel-to-place window.
> - Row 5 changes stop timing, so it ships as LATE_FILL_EXPECT_MODE="shadow". It snapshots the cache before the re-protect, and the broker reads and rows are buffered until after the reconcile loop, so no order's stop waits on them.
> Out of scope (do not touch): rows 11-13 (noted on the roadmap), enforcing row 5.
> Acceptance check: shadow never changes the re-protect's arguments; a stale-high count never sizes above held − fill; alerts come after the place; suite green; two fresh reviews clean.
> Open questions for Luke: none (stop-path engineering under the standing approval of 11-16; enforce is a later decision after the shadow rows are read).

Their SHOULD-FIXes (buffer the shadow rows after the reconcile loop; read the cache directly, without a reconnect) are in 3188c32, delta-checked by the lead.
